<script>
        var excludingJS = 1;

        function checkAllowSessionStorage() {
            var test = 'test';
            try {
                sessionStorage.setItem(test, test);
                sessionStorage.removeItem(test);
                return true;
            } catch(e) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if (checkAllowSessionStorage() === true) {
            console.log('checkAllowSessionStorage | allowed');
            allowSessionStorage = true;
        } else {
            console.log('checkAllowSessionStorage | denied');
            allowSessionStorage = false;
        }
    </script>
<!-- Cookie Functions -->
<script type="application/javascript">
        console.log('init cookieFuncs');

        var excludedOptimizeJS = excludedOptimizeJS || [];
        excludedOptimizeJS.push('cookieFuncs');

        if ('undefined' === typeof readCookie) {
            window.readCookie = function(name) {
                var nameEQ = name + "=";
                var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
                for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                    var c = ca[i];
                    while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
                    if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
                }
                return null;
            }
        }

        if ('undefined' === typeof createCookie) {
            window.createCookie = function(name, value, days) {
                if (days) {
                    var date = new Date();
                    date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                    var expires = "; expires=" + date.toGMTString();
                } else {
                    var expires = "";
                }

                document.cookie = name + "=" + value + expires + "; path=/";
            }
        }

        if ('undefined' === typeof pencilClick) {
            window.pencilClick = function () {};
        }
    </script>

            var ex_class_array = document.getElementsByClassName(ex_class);
            for (j = 0; j < ex_class_array.length; j++) {
                 var src = ex_class_array[j].getAttribute("data-src") ? ex_class_array[j].getAttribute("data-src") : ex_class_array[j].src ;
            }
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener("scroll", function(event){
         var top = this.scrollY;
         lazyloadimages(top);
         lazyloadiframes(top);
         
    });
    
    function vimeoLoadGaData() {
        var vimeoObj = this;
        var iframe   = vimeoObj.element;
        var label    = iframe.getAttribute("data-ga-label");
        var page     = "string" === typeof gaPageName && "" != gaPageName ? gaPageName : "Unknown Page";
        
        vimeoObj.getVideoId().then(function (videoId) {
            vimeoObj.sessKey = videoId + "_" + page.replace(" | ", "_");
            vimeoObj.sessKey = vimeoObj.sessKey.toLowerCase();
        });
        
        if (label) {        
            vimeoObj.gaLabel = page + " | " + label;
        } else {
            vimeoObj.getVideoTitle().then(function (title) {                
                vimeoObj.gaLabel = page + " | " + title;
            });
        }
        
        vimeoObj.gaEventStates = {
            play: false,
            end: false
        };
    }
    
    function vimeoTriggerGaEvent(action) {
        var vimeoObj = this;
        var iframe   = vimeoObj.element;
        var actions  = "play|end";
        var cat      = "PTP-Videos";
        var videoId  = iframe.getAttribute("id");
        
        if ("function" !== typeof ga) {
            console.error("[vimeoTriggerGaEvent] ga is not a function");
            return false;
        }
        
        if ( ! action) {
            console.error("[vimeoTriggerGaEvent] undefined action");
            return false;
        }
        
        action = action.toLowerCase();
    
        if (actions.indexOf(action) < 0) {
            console.error("[vimeoTriggerGaEvent] invalid action: " + action);
            return false;
        }
        
        var sessKey = "vimeo_" + vimeoObj.sessKey + "_" + action;
        
        if (this.gaEventStates[action] || sessionStorage.getItem(sessKey)) {
            console.log("[vimeoTriggerGaEvent] [" + videoId + "] [" + action + "] has already been triggered!", this.gaEventStates[action], sessionStorage.getItem(sessKey));
            return false;
        } else {
            console.log("[vimeoTriggerGaEvent] [" + videoId + "] [" + action + "] | start tracking event");
        }
    
        //console.log(vimeoObj.gaLabel);
        this.gaEventStates[action] = true;
        sessionStorage.setItem(sessKey, true);
    
        if (isLiveServer) {
            ga("send", "event", cat, action, vimeoObj.gaLabel);
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    function loadVimeoAPI() {
        if (1 == is_vimeo_api_loaded) {
            return;
        }
        
        if (1 == is_vimeo_api_loading) {    
            if ("object" === typeof Vimeo) {
                is_vimeo_api_loaded = 1;
                clearInterval(itvVimeo);
                
                Vimeo.Player.prototype.loadGaData = vimeoLoadGaData;
                Vimeo.Player.prototype.triggerGaEvent = vimeoTriggerGaEvent;
                
                Vimeo.Player.prototype.triggerGaPlay = function () {
                    this.triggerGaEvent("play");
                };
                
                Vimeo.Player.prototype.triggerGaEnd  = function () {
                    this.triggerGaEvent("end");
                }
                
                loadVimeoApiForNoLazyVideos();
                
                //console.log("endLoadingVimeoApi");
            }
            return;
        }
        
        //console.log("beginLoadingVimeoAPI");
        
        var tagVimeo, firstScriptTag; 
        
        tagVimeo       = document.createElement("script");
        tagVimeo.src   = "https://player.vimeo.com/api/player.js";
        firstScriptTag = document.getElementsByTagName("script")[0];
        
        if ("object" === typeof firstScriptTag) {
            firstScriptTag.parentNode.insertBefore(tagVimeo, firstScriptTag);
            is_vimeo_api_loading = 1;
        }
    }
    
    function vimeoSetDefaultTextTrack(vimeoObj) {
        if ("function" === typeof vimeoObj.getTextTracks && "function" === typeof vimeoObj.enableTextTrack) {
            vimeoObj.getTextTracks().then(function (textTracks) {
            
                if ("object" === typeof textTracks && textTracks.length > 0) {
                    if ("string" === typeof curLangCode && "en" != curLangCode) {
                        vimeoObj.disableTextTrack();
                    } else {
                        vimeoObj.enableTextTrack(textTracks[0].language);
                    }
                }
            });
            
            return true;
        }
        
        return false;
    }
    
    function loadApiForVimeoIframe(videoObj, autoPlay) {
        if ("object" !== typeof Vimeo || "object" !== typeof videoObj || "1" == videoObj.getAttribute("data-vimeo-loaded")) {
            return false;
        }
        
        var id = videoObj.getAttribute("id");    
        var vimeoObj = new Vimeo.Player(videoObj);
        
        vimeoObj.getVideoId().catch(function (err) {
            var altVideo = videoObj.getAttribute("alt-video");
            
            if ("string" === typeof altVideo && "" !== altVideo) {
                videoObj.setAttribute("src", altVideo);
                videoObj.setAttribute("use-alt-video", "true");
            }
        });
        
        vimeoObj.setLoop(false);
        vimeoObj.loadGaData();
        vimeoObj.on("loaded", function () {
            console.log("loaded vimeo! [" + id + "]");
            //onVimeoStateChange(id, false);
            
            if (autoPlay) {
                vimeoObj.play();
            }
        });
        vimeoObj.on("bufferstart", function () {
            this.triggerGaPlay();
            console.log("bufferstart vimeo! [" + id + "]");
            onVimeoStateChange(id, true);
        });
        vimeoObj.on("play", function () {
            this.triggerGaPlay();
            console.log("played vimeo! [" + id + "]");
            onVimeoStateChange(id, true);
        });
        vimeoObj.on("pause", function () {
            console.log("paused vimeo! [" + id + "]");
            onVimeoStateChange(id, false);
        });
        vimeoObj.on("ended", function () {
            this.triggerGaEnd();
            console.log("ended vimeo! [" + id + "]");
            onVimeoStateChange(id, false);
        });
        
        Player[id] = vimeoObj;
        
        videoObj.setAttribute("data-vimeo-loaded", "1");
        
        if ( ! vimeoSetDefaultTextTrack(vimeoObj)) {
            Player[id]["itvSetTextTrack"] = setInterval(function () {
                console.log("itvSetTextTrack", "vimeo_id_" + id);
                if (vimeoSetDefaultTextTrack(Player[id])) {
                    clearInterval(Player[id]["itvSetTextTrack"]);
                }
            }, 1000);
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    function loadVimeoApiForNoLazyVideos() {
        var videos = document.querySelectorAll('iframe[class*="no-lazy"][src*="player.vimeo.com"]:not([data-vimeo-loaded="1"])');
        
        console.log("loadVimeoApiForNoLazyVideos", videos.length);
        
        if (videos.length < 1) {
            return;
        }
        
        for (i=0; i < videos.length; i++) {
            loadApiForVimeoIframe(videos[i]);
        };
    }
    
    function loadYouTubeIframeAPI() {
        var tag = document.createElement("script");

        tag.src = "https://www.youtube.com/iframe_api";
        var firstScriptTag = document.getElementsByTagName("script")[0];
        firstScriptTag.parentNode.insertBefore(tag, firstScriptTag);
        
        is_iframe_api_loading = 1;
    }
    
    function loadHighslide() {
        var tag = document.createElement("script");

        tag.src = "https://www.paintyourlife.com/highslide/highslide-full.min.js";
        var firstScriptTag = document.getElementsByTagName("script")[0];
        firstScriptTag.parentNode.insertBefore(tag, firstScriptTag);
        
        loading_highslide = 1;
        
        setTimeout(function () {
            loading_highslide = 0;
            loaded_highslide = 1;
        }, 2000);
    }
    
    var loading_highslide = 0, loaded_highslide = 0;
    var is_iframe_api_loading = 0;
    var is_vimeo_api_loading = 0;
    var is_vimeo_api_loaded = 0;
    var is_iframe_api = 0;
    var Player = {};
    
    window.onscroll = function () {
        if (0 == is_iframe_api && 0 == is_iframe_api_loading) {
            loadYouTubeIframeAPI();
        }
        
        if (0 === loaded_highslide && 0 === loading_highslide) {
            loadHighslide();
        }
    }
    
     function onYouTubeIframeAPIReady() {
        is_iframe_api = 1;
        is_iframe_api_loading = 0;
     }
    function lazyload_img(imgs,bodyRect,window_height,win_width){
        for (i = 0; i < imgs.length; i++) {
            
            if(imgs[i].getAttribute("data-class") == "LazyLoad"){
                var elemRect = imgs[i].getBoundingClientRect(),
                offset   = elemRect.top - bodyRect.top;
/*console.log(elemRect.top+" - "+window_height+"  -  "+imgs[i].getAttribute("data-src"));*/
                if(elemRect.top != 0 && elemRect.top - window_height < 0 ){
                    var src = imgs[i].getAttribute("data-src") ? imgs[i].getAttribute("data-src") : imgs[i].src ;
                    var srcset = imgs[i].getAttribute("data-srcset") ? imgs[i].getAttribute("data-srcset") : "";
                    imgs[i].src = src;
                    if(imgs[i].srcset != null & imgs[i].srcset != ""){
                        imgs[i].srcset = srcset;
                    }
                    delete imgs[i].dataset.class;
                    imgs[i].setAttribute("data-done","Loaded");
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    function lazyload_iframe(imgs,bodyRect,window_height,win_width){
        for (i = 0; i < imgs.length; i++) {
            var isVimeo = "vimeo" === imgs[i].getAttribute("data-video-provider");
            
            if (isVimeo) {
                if (0 == is_vimeo_api_loaded) {
                    return;
                }
            } else if (is_iframe_api == 0) {
                return;
            }    
            
            if(imgs[i].getAttribute("data-class") == "LazyLoad"){
                var elemRect = imgs[i].getBoundingClientRect(),
                offset   = elemRect.top - bodyRect.top;
                /*console.log(elemRect.top+" - "+window_height+"  -  "+imgs[i].getAttribute("data-src"));*/
                if(elemRect.top != 0 && elemRect.top - window_height < 0 ){
                    var src = imgs[i].getAttribute("data-src") ? imgs[i].getAttribute("data-src") : imgs[i].src ;
                    var srcset = imgs[i].getAttribute("data-srcset") ? imgs[i].getAttribute("data-srcset") : "";
                    imgs[i].src = src;
                    if(imgs[i].srcset != null & imgs[i].srcset != ""){
                        imgs[i].srcset = srcset;
                    }
                    delete imgs[i].dataset.class;
                    imgs[i].setAttribute("data-done","Loaded");
                    var id = imgs[i].getAttribute("id");

                    if(id != null){
                        if (isVimeo) {
                            loadApiForVimeoIframe(imgs[i]);
                        } else {
                            Player[i] = new YT.Player(imgs[i], {
                                events: {
                                  "onReady": onPlayerReady,
                                  "onStateChange": onPlayerStateChange
                                }
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    function onPlayerReady(event) {
        console.log("ready");
    }
    
    function onVimeoStateChange(videoId, play) {
        window.videoObj = document.getElementById(videoId);
   
        if (play) {
            if ("null" !== window.videoObj) {
                videoObj.setAttribute("data-playing", "1");
            }
            
            document.cookie = "playing=yes";
        } else {
            if ("null" !== window.videoObj) {
                videoObj.setAttribute("data-playing", "0");
            }
            
            document.cookie = "playing=no";
        }
    }

    function onPlayerStateChange(event) {
        console.log("[youtube_iframe] onPlayerStateChange", event);
        if(event.data == 1 || event.data == 3){
            event.target.a.setAttribute("data-playing","1");
        }else{
            event.target.a.setAttribute("data-playing","0");
        }
        var iframes = document.getElementsByTagName("iframe");
         var playing = "no";
         for (i = 0; i < iframes.length; i++) {
            var is_playing = iframes[i].getAttribute("data-playing");
            if(1 == is_playing){
                playing = "yes";
                break;
            }
         }
         document.cookie = "playing="+playing;
    }
    
      function lazyload_video(imgs,bodyRect,window_height,win_width){
        for (i = 0; i < imgs.length; i++) {
            var source = imgs[i].getElementsByTagName("source")[0];
            
           
            if ("object" === typeof source) {
                if("function" === typeof source.getAttribute && source.getAttribute("data-class") == "LazyLoad"){
                    var elemRect = imgs[i].getBoundingClientRect(),
                    offset   = elemRect.top - bodyRect.top;
    
                    if(elemRect.top - window_height < 0 ){
                        var src = source.getAttribute("data-src") ? source.getAttribute("data-src") : source.src ;
                        var srcset = source.getAttribute("data-srcset") ? source.getAttribute("data-srcset") : "";
                        imgs[i].src = src;
                        if(source.srcset != null & source.srcset != ""){
                            source.srcset = srcset;
                        }
                        delete source.dataset.class;
                        source.setAttribute("data-done","Loaded");
                    }
                }
            } 
        }
    }
    function lazyloadimages(top){
        var imgs = document.getElementsByTagName("img");
        
        var sources = document.getElementsByTagName("video");
        var bodyRect = document.body.getBoundingClientRect();
        var window_height = window.innerHeight;
        var win_width = screen.availWidth;
        lazyload_img(imgs,bodyRect,window_height,win_width);
        lazyload_video(sources,bodyRect,window_height,win_width);
    }
    function lazyloadiframes(top){
        var bodyRect = document.body.getBoundingClientRect();
        var window_height = window.innerHeight;
        var win_width = screen.availWidth;
        var iframes = document.getElementsByTagName("iframe");
        lazyload_iframe(iframes,bodyRect,window_height,win_width);
    }
    
    function send_images_to_optimize(imgs_data){
        var images_str = JSON.stringify(imgs_data);
        var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
        xhttp.onreadystatechange = function() {
            if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200) {
                console.log(this.response);
           }
        };
        xhttp.open("POST", "https://www.paintyourlife.com/optimization.php?set-opt=1", true);
        xhttp.setRequestHeader("Content-type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded");
        xhttp.send("images123="+images_str);
    }
